5. Create Slug Helper
Create file: Helpers/SlugHelper.cs

csharp
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace MediCare.API.Helpers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Generates URL-friendly slugs from text.
    /// Example: "Napa Syrup 100ml" → "napa-syrup-100ml"
    /// </summary>
    public static class SlugHelper
    {
        public static string GenerateSlug(string text)
        {
            // Convert to lowercase
            var slug = text.ToLowerInvariant();

            // Replace spaces and special chars with hyphens
            slug = Regex.Replace(slug, @"[^a-z0-9\s-]", "");
            slug = Regex.Replace(slug, @"\s+", "-");
            slug = Regex.Replace(slug, @"-+", "-");

            // Trim hyphens from start and end
            slug = slug.Trim('-');

            return slug;
        }

        public static string GenerateUniqueSlug(string text, string uniqueSuffix)
        {
            var baseSlug = GenerateSlug(text);
            return $"{baseSlug}-{uniqueSuffix}";
        }
    }
}
6. Create Category Service
Create file: Services/CategoryService.cs

csharp
using Mapster;
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Category;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles all category CRUD operations.
    /// Supports nested categories via ParentCategoryId.
    /// </summary>
    public class CategoryService : ICategoryService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public CategoryService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<CategoryResponseDto>>> GetAllAsync()
        {
            var categories = await _context.Categories
                .Include(c => c.SubCategories)
                .Where(c => c.ParentCategoryId == null && c.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(c => c.SortOrder)
                .ToListAsync();

            var result = categories.Adapt<List<CategoryResponseDto>>();
            return ApiResponseDto<List<CategoryResponseDto>>.Ok(result);
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>> GetByIdAsync(int id)
        {
            var category = await _context.Categories
                .Include(c => c.SubCategories)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == id);

            if (category is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Category not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Ok(
                category.Adapt<CategoryResponseDto>());
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>> CreateAsync(
            CategoryRequestDto request)
        {
            // Check if parent exists
            if (request.ParentCategoryId.HasValue)
            {
                var parentExists = await _context.Categories
                    .AnyAsync(c => c.Id == request.ParentCategoryId.Value);

                if (!parentExists)
                    return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Fail(
                        "Parent category not found.");
            }

            var category = new Category
            {
                Name             = request.Name.Trim(),
                Slug             = SlugHelper.GenerateUniqueSlug(
                                       request.Name,
                                       Guid.NewGuid().ToString()[..6]),
                ImageUrl         = request.ImageUrl,
                Description      = request.Description,
                ParentCategoryId = request.ParentCategoryId,
                SortOrder        = request.SortOrder,
                IsActive         = request.IsActive
            };

            _context.Categories.Add(category);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Ok(
                category.Adapt<CategoryResponseDto>(),
                "Category created successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>> UpdateAsync(
            int id, CategoryRequestDto request)
        {
            var category = await _context.Categories.FindAsync(id);

            if (category is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Category not found.");

            category.Name             = request.Name.Trim();
            category.ImageUrl         = request.ImageUrl;
            category.Description      = request.Description;
            category.ParentCategoryId = request.ParentCategoryId;
            category.SortOrder        = request.SortOrder;
            category.IsActive         = request.IsActive;

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<CategoryResponseDto>.Ok(
                category.Adapt<CategoryResponseDto>(),
                "Category updated successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> DeleteAsync(int id)
        {
            var category = await _context.Categories
                .Include(c => c.Products)
                .Include(c => c.SubCategories)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == id);

            if (category is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Category not found.");

            if (category.Products.Any())
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Cannot delete category — it has products assigned to it.");

            if (category.SubCategories.Any())
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Cannot delete category — it has subcategories.");

            _context.Categories.Remove(category);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true, "Category deleted successfully.");
        }
    }
}
7. Create Brand Service
Create file: Services/BrandService.cs

csharp
using Mapster;
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Brand;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles all brand CRUD operations.
    /// </summary>
    public class BrandService : IBrandService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public BrandService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<BrandResponseDto>>> GetAllAsync()
        {
            var brands = await _context.Brands
                .Where(b => b.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(b => b.Name)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<BrandResponseDto>>.Ok(
                brands.Adapt<List<BrandResponseDto>>());
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>> GetByIdAsync(int id)
        {
            var brand = await _context.Brands.FindAsync(id);

            if (brand is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Fail("Brand not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Ok(
                brand.Adapt<BrandResponseDto>());
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>> CreateAsync(
            BrandRequestDto request)
        {
            var nameExists = await _context.Brands
                .AnyAsync(b => b.Name.ToLower() == request.Name.ToLower().Trim());

            if (nameExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "A brand with this name already exists.");

            var brand = new Brand
            {
                Name        = request.Name.Trim(),
                Slug        = SlugHelper.GenerateUniqueSlug(
                                  request.Name,
                                  Guid.NewGuid().ToString()[..6]),
                LogoUrl     = request.LogoUrl,
                Description = request.Description,
                IsActive    = request.IsActive
            };

            _context.Brands.Add(brand);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Ok(
                brand.Adapt<BrandResponseDto>(),
                "Brand created successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>> UpdateAsync(
            int id, BrandRequestDto request)
        {
            var brand = await _context.Brands.FindAsync(id);

            if (brand is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Fail("Brand not found.");

            brand.Name        = request.Name.Trim();
            brand.LogoUrl     = request.LogoUrl;
            brand.Description = request.Description;
            brand.IsActive    = request.IsActive;

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<BrandResponseDto>.Ok(
                brand.Adapt<BrandResponseDto>(),
                "Brand updated successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> DeleteAsync(int id)
        {
            var brand = await _context.Brands
                .Include(b => b.Products)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(b => b.Id == id);

            if (brand is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Brand not found.");

            if (brand.Products.Any())
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Cannot delete brand — it has products assigned to it.");

            _context.Brands.Remove(brand);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true, "Brand deleted successfully.");
        }
    }
}
8. Create Product Service
Create file: Services/ProductService.cs

csharp
using Mapster;
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Product;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles all product operations including
    /// search, filter, pagination and CRUD.
    /// </summary>
    public class ProductService : IProductService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public ProductService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        // ── Base query with all includes ──────────────────────
        private IQueryable<Product> BaseQuery() =>
            _context.Products
                .Include(p => p.Category)
                .Include(p => p.Brand)
                .Include(p => p.Images)
                .Include(p => p.Reviews)
                .AsNoTracking();

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<ProductListResponseDto>>>
            GetAllAsync(ProductFilterDto filter)
        {
            var query = BaseQuery().Where(p => p.IsActive);

            // ── Search ────────────────────────────────────────
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filter.Search))
            {
                var search = filter.Search.ToLower().Trim();
                query = query.Where(p =>
                    p.Name.ToLower().Contains(search) ||
                    (p.GenericName != null &&
                     p.GenericName.ToLower().Contains(search)) ||
                    (p.ActiveIngredient != null &&
                     p.ActiveIngredient.ToLower().Contains(search)) ||
                    p.Brand.Name.ToLower().Contains(search));
            }

            // ── Filters ───────────────────────────────────────
            if (filter.CategoryId.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(p => p.CategoryId == filter.CategoryId.Value);

            if (filter.BrandId.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(p => p.BrandId == filter.BrandId.Value);

            if (filter.MinPrice.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(p => p.Price >= filter.MinPrice.Value);

            if (filter.MaxPrice.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(p => p.Price <= filter.MaxPrice.Value);

            if (filter.RequiresPrescription.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(p =>
                    p.RequiresPrescription == filter.RequiresPrescription.Value);

            if (filter.IsInStock == true)
                query = query.Where(p => p.Stock > 0);

            if (filter.IsFeatured == true)
                query = query.Where(p => p.IsFeatured);

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filter.DosageForm))
                query = query.Where(p => p.DosageForm == filter.DosageForm);

            // ── Sorting ───────────────────────────────────────
            query = filter.SortBy switch
            {
                "price_asc"  => query.OrderBy(p => p.Price),
                "price_desc" => query.OrderByDescending(p => p.Price),
                "newest"     => query.OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt),
                _            => query.OrderByDescending(p => p.IsFeatured)
                                     .ThenByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt)
            };

            // ── Pagination ────────────────────────────────────
            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();
            var items      = await query
                .Skip((filter.PageNumber - 1) * filter.PageSize)
                .Take(filter.PageSize)
                .ToListAsync();

            var mapped = items.Select(p =>
            {
                var dto              = p.Adapt<ProductListResponseDto>();
                dto.AverageRating    = p.Reviews.Any()
                    ? Math.Round(p.Reviews.Average(r => r.Rating), 1) : 0;
                dto.ReviewCount      = p.Reviews.Count;
                dto.IsLowStock       = p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold
                                       && p.Stock > 0;
                dto.PrimaryImageUrl  = p.Images
                    .FirstOrDefault(i => i.IsPrimary)?.ImageUrl
                    ?? p.Images.FirstOrDefault()?.ImageUrl;
                return dto;
            }).ToList();

            var paged = new PagedResponseDto<ProductListResponseDto>
            {
                Items       = mapped,
                TotalCount  = totalCount,
                PageNumber  = filter.PageNumber,
                PageSize    = filter.PageSize
            };

            return ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<ProductListResponseDto>>.Ok(paged);
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>> GetBySlugAsync(string slug)
        {
            var product = await BaseQuery()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Slug == slug && p.IsActive);

            if (product is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail("Product not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapToFullDto(product));
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>> GetByIdAsync(int id)
        {
            var product = await BaseQuery()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == id);

            if (product is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail("Product not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapToFullDto(product));
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>> CreateAsync(
            ProductRequestDto request)
        {
            // ── Validate category ─────────────────────────────
            var categoryExists = await _context.Categories
                .AnyAsync(c => c.Id == request.CategoryId && c.IsActive);

            if (!categoryExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Category not found or inactive.");

            // ── Validate brand ────────────────────────────────
            var brandExists = await _context.Brands
                .AnyAsync(b => b.Id == request.BrandId && b.IsActive);

            if (!brandExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Brand not found or inactive.");

            // ── Generate unique SKU ───────────────────────────
            var sku = $"MED-{Guid.NewGuid().ToString()[..8].ToUpper()}";

            // ── Generate unique slug ──────────────────────────
            var slug = SlugHelper.GenerateUniqueSlug(
                request.Name,
                Guid.NewGuid().ToString()[..6]);

            var product = new Product
            {
                Name                  = request.Name.Trim(),
                Slug                  = slug,
                SKU                   = sku,
                Description           = request.Description,
                ShortDescription      = request.ShortDescription,
                CategoryId            = request.CategoryId,
                BrandId               = request.BrandId,
                Price                 = request.Price,
                DiscountPrice         = request.DiscountPrice,
                Stock                 = request.Stock,
                LowStockThreshold     = request.LowStockThreshold,
                RequiresPrescription  = request.RequiresPrescription,
                DosageForm            = request.DosageForm,
                Strength              = request.Strength,
                PackSize              = request.PackSize,
                GenericName           = request.GenericName,
                Manufacturer          = request.Manufacturer,
                ActiveIngredient      = request.ActiveIngredient,
                Indications           = request.Indications,
                SideEffects           = request.SideEffects,
                Warnings              = request.Warnings,
                Contraindications     = request.Contraindications,
                StorageInfo           = request.StorageInfo,
                PregnancyWarning      = request.PregnancyWarning,
                ChildSafetyInfo       = request.ChildSafetyInfo,
                IsActive              = request.IsActive,
                IsFeatured            = request.IsFeatured
            };

            _context.Products.Add(product);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Return with full includes ─────────────────────
            var created = await BaseQuery()
                .FirstAsync(p => p.Id == product.Id);

            return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapToFullDto(created),
                "Product created successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>> UpdateAsync(
            int id, ProductRequestDto request)
        {
            var product = await _context.Products.FindAsync(id);

            if (product is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail("Product not found.");

            // ── Validate category ─────────────────────────────
            var categoryExists = await _context.Categories
                .AnyAsync(c => c.Id == request.CategoryId && c.IsActive);

            if (!categoryExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Category not found or inactive.");

            // ── Validate brand ────────────────────────────────
            var brandExists = await _context.Brands
                .AnyAsync(b => b.Id == request.BrandId && b.IsActive);

            if (!brandExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Brand not found or inactive.");

            // ── Update fields ─────────────────────────────────
            product.Name                 = request.Name.Trim();
            product.Description          = request.Description;
            product.ShortDescription     = request.ShortDescription;
            product.CategoryId           = request.CategoryId;
            product.BrandId              = request.BrandId;
            product.Price                = request.Price;
            product.DiscountPrice        = request.DiscountPrice;
            product.Stock                = request.Stock;
            product.LowStockThreshold    = request.LowStockThreshold;
            product.RequiresPrescription = request.RequiresPrescription;
            product.DosageForm           = request.DosageForm;
            product.Strength             = request.Strength;
            product.PackSize             = request.PackSize;
            product.GenericName          = request.GenericName;
            product.Manufacturer         = request.Manufacturer;
            product.ActiveIngredient     = request.ActiveIngredient;
            product.Indications          = request.Indications;
            product.SideEffects          = request.SideEffects;
            product.Warnings             = request.Warnings;
            product.Contraindications    = request.Contraindications;
            product.StorageInfo          = request.StorageInfo;
            product.PregnancyWarning     = request.PregnancyWarning;
            product.ChildSafetyInfo      = request.ChildSafetyInfo;
            product.IsActive             = request.IsActive;
            product.IsFeatured           = request.IsFeatured;

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            var updated = await BaseQuery()
                .FirstAsync(p => p.Id == product.Id);

            return ApiResponseDto<ProductResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapToFullDto(updated),
                "Product updated successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> DeleteAsync(int id)
        {
            var product = await _context.Products
                .Include(p => p.OrderItems)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == id);

            if (product is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Product not found.");

            // ── Soft delete if product has orders ─────────────
            if (product.OrderItems.Any())
            {
                product.IsActive = false;
                await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                    "Product deactivated — it has existing orders.");
            }

            _context.Products.Remove(product);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Product deleted successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>>
            GetFeaturedAsync()
        {
            var products = await BaseQuery()
                .Where(p => p.IsActive && p.IsFeatured)
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt)
                .Take(10)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>.Ok(
                products.Select(p => MapToListDto(p)).ToList());
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>>
            GetLowStockAsync()
        {
            var products = await _context.Products
                .Where(p => p.IsActive && p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold)
                .OrderBy(p => p.Stock)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>.Ok(
                products.Adapt<List<ProductListResponseDto>>());
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>>
            GetExpiringAsync(int days)
        {
            var expiryDate = DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(days);

            var batches = await _context.InventoryBatches
                .Include(b => b.Product)
                .Where(b => b.ExpiryDate <= expiryDate
                            && b.Quantity > 0
                            && b.Product.IsActive)
                .Select(b => b.Product)
                .Distinct()
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<ProductListResponseDto>>.Ok(
                batches.Adapt<List<ProductListResponseDto>>());
        }

        // ── Private mapping helpers ───────────────────────────
        private static ProductResponseDto MapToFullDto(Product p)
        {
            var dto             = p.Adapt<ProductResponseDto>();
            dto.AverageRating   = p.Reviews.Any()
                ? Math.Round(p.Reviews.Average(r => r.Rating), 1) : 0;
            dto.ReviewCount     = p.Reviews.Count;
            dto.ImageUrls       = p.Images.Select(i => i.ImageUrl).ToList();
            dto.IsLowStock      = p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold && p.Stock > 0;
            return dto;
        }

        private static ProductListResponseDto MapToListDto(Product p)
        {
            var dto             = p.Adapt<ProductListResponseDto>();
            dto.AverageRating   = p.Reviews.Any()
                ? Math.Round(p.Reviews.Average(r => r.Rating), 1) : 0;
            dto.ReviewCount     = p.Reviews.Count;
            dto.IsLowStock      = p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold && p.Stock > 0;
            dto.PrimaryImageUrl = p.Images
                .FirstOrDefault(i => i.IsPrimary)?.ImageUrl
                ?? p.Images.FirstOrDefault()?.ImageUrl;
            return dto;
        }
    }
}
9. Create Category Controller
Create file: Controllers/CategoryController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Category;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages product categories.
    /// GET endpoints are public.
    /// POST, PUT, DELETE require Admin role.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/categories")]
    public class CategoryController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ICategoryService _categoryService;

        public CategoryController(ICategoryService categoryService)
        {
            _categoryService = categoryService;
        }

        /// <summary>Get all active categories with subcategories.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAll()
        {
            var result = await _categoryService.GetAllAsync();
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get a single category by ID.</summary>
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(int id)
        {
            var result = await _categoryService.GetByIdAsync(id);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Create a new category. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpPost]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
            [FromBody] CategoryRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _categoryService.CreateAsync(request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Update an existing category. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
            int id, [FromBody] CategoryRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _categoryService.UpdateAsync(id, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Delete a category. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
        {
            var result = await _categoryService.DeleteAsync(id);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }
    }
}
10. Create Brand Controller
Create file: Controllers/BrandController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Brand;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages medicine brands/manufacturers.
    /// GET endpoints are public.
    /// POST, PUT, DELETE require Admin role.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/brands")]
    public class BrandController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly IBrandService _brandService;

        public BrandController(IBrandService brandService)
        {
            _brandService = brandService;
        }

        /// <summary>Get all active brands.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAll()
        {
            var result = await _brandService.GetAllAsync();
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get a single brand by ID.</summary>
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(int id)
        {
            var result = await _brandService.GetByIdAsync(id);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Create a new brand. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpPost]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
            [FromBody] BrandRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _brandService.CreateAsync(request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Update an existing brand. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
            int id, [FromBody] BrandRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _brandService.UpdateAsync(id, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Delete a brand. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
        {
            var result = await _brandService.DeleteAsync(id);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }
    }
}

using MediCare.API.DTOs.Product;

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers

    /// Manages the medicine product catalog.

        public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
            [FromBody] ProductRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _productService.CreateAsync(request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Update an existing product. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
            int id, [FromBody] ProductRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _productService.UpdateAsync(id, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Delete a product. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("{id:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
        {
            var result = await _productService.DeleteAsync(id);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);

Apr 2
Claude responded: The error is clear:
The error is clear:

Column 'UpdatedAt' is null
System.InvalidCastException
The UpdatedAt column in the database is NULL but our BaseEntity has it as DateTime? — which should handle nulls. The issue is that Npgsql on .NET 10 is strict about nullable DateTime mapping.

Fix — Update BaseEntity.cs
Open Models/BaseEntity.cs and make sure UpdatedAt is nullable:

csharp
namespace MediCare.API.Models
{
    public abstract class BaseEntity
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;
        public DateTime? UpdatedAt { get; set; }  // ← must be nullable
    }
}
Fix — Update AppDbContext.cs
The real fix is telling EF Core that UpdatedAt is optional in the database. Add this to OnModelCreating in AppDbContext.cs — right after base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);:

csharp
// ── Global nullable DateTime configuration ────────────────
foreach (var entityType in modelBuilder.Model.GetEntityTypes())
{
    foreach (var property in entityType.GetProperties())
    {
        if (property.ClrType == typeof(DateTime?))
        {
            property.IsNullable = true;
        }
    }
}
Fix — Update the SQL table for the Admin user
The admin user you inserted via SQL has UpdatedAt as NULL — that is correct since it was never updated. But the issue is the column type in PostgreSQL vs what EF Core expects.

Run this in pgAdmin to fix the column type:

sql
ALTER TABLE "Users" 
ALTER COLUMN "UpdatedAt" TYPE TIMESTAMP;
Quickest fix — just update the Admin user to have a non-null UpdatedAt:
Run in pgAdmin:

sql
UPDATE "Users" 
SET "UpdatedAt" = NOW() 
WHERE "UpdatedAt" IS NULL;

UPDATE "Carts"
SET "UpdatedAt" = NOW()
WHERE "UpdatedAt" IS NULL;
Then run again:
bash
dotnet run
Test login:

json
{
  "email": "admin@medicare.com",
  "password": "Admin@1234"
}
Why this happens
When we created the admin user manually via SQL — we did not set UpdatedAt so it is NULL in the database. EF Core with Npgsql on .NET 10 is very strict — it tries to read NULL as DateTime (non-nullable) and crashes.

The UPDATE query above sets UpdatedAt = NOW() for all existing rows which fixes it immediately.

Run the UPDATE query in pgAdmin and try login again! 🚀

Step 7 — Cart & Wishlist API
We will build the complete cart and wishlist system. Cart is per user, persists in database, and blocks Rx items without an approved prescription.

1. Create Cart Interface
Create file: Interfaces/ICartService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Cart;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;

namespace MediCare.API.Interfaces
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Contract for cart operations.
    /// All operations require authenticated customer.
    /// </summary>
    public interface ICartService
    {
        Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> GetCartAsync(int userId);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> AddToCartAsync(int userId, AddToCartRequestDto request);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> UpdateQuantityAsync(int userId, int cartItemId, int quantity);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> RemoveItemAsync(int userId, int cartItemId);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> ClearCartAsync(int userId);
    }
}

3. Create a Helper to get UserId from Token
Create file: Helpers/ClaimsPrincipalExtensions.cs

csharp
using System.Security.Claims;

namespace MediCare.API.Helpers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Extension methods to extract user data from JWT claims.
    /// Used in controllers to get the logged-in user's ID.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ClaimsPrincipalExtensions
    {
        public static int GetUserId(this ClaimsPrincipal user)
        {
            var claim = user.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)
                     ?? user.FindFirst("uid");

            if (claim is null)
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                    "User ID claim not found in token.");

            return int.Parse(claim.Value);
        }

        public static string GetUserRole(this ClaimsPrincipal user)
        {
            return user.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.Role)?.Value ?? string.Empty;
        }

        public static string GetUserEmail(this ClaimsPrincipal user)
        {
            return user.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.Email)?.Value ?? string.Empty;
        }
    }
}
4. Create Cart Service
Create file: Services/CartService.cs

csharp
using Mapster;
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Cart;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles all cart operations.
    /// One cart per user — created automatically on registration.
    /// Validates stock and prescription requirements on add.
    /// </summary>
    public class CartService : ICartService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public CartService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> GetCartAsync(int userId)
        {
            var cart = await GetCartWithItemsAsync(userId);

            if (cart is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail("Cart not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Ok(MapCart(cart));
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> AddToCartAsync(
            int userId, AddToCartRequestDto request)
        {
            // ── Validate product exists and is active ─────────
            var product = await _context.Products
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == request.ProductId
                                       && p.IsActive);

            if (product is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Product not found or unavailable.");

            // ── Check stock ───────────────────────────────────
            if (product.Stock < request.Quantity)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                    $"Insufficient stock. Only {product.Stock} units available.");

            // ── Get or create cart ────────────────────────────
            var cart = await _context.Carts
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.UserId == userId);

            if (cart is null)
            {
                cart = new Cart { UserId = userId };
                _context.Carts.Add(cart);
                await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            // ── Check if item already in cart ─────────────────
            var existingItem = await _context.CartItems
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ci => ci.CartId    == cart.Id
                                        && ci.ProductId == request.ProductId);

            if (existingItem is not null)
            {
                // Update quantity
                var newQuantity = existingItem.Quantity + request.Quantity;

                if (product.Stock < newQuantity)
                    return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                        $"Cannot add more. Only {product.Stock} units available.");

                existingItem.Quantity = newQuantity;
            }
            else
            {
                // Add new cart item
                var cartItem = new CartItem
                {
                    CartId    = cart.Id,
                    ProductId = request.ProductId,
                    Quantity  = request.Quantity
                };
                _context.CartItems.Add(cartItem);
            }

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Return updated cart ───────────────────────────
            var updatedCart = await GetCartWithItemsAsync(userId);
            return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapCart(updatedCart!),
                "Item added to cart.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>> UpdateQuantityAsync(
            int userId, int cartItemId, int quantity)
        {
            // ── Get cart item and verify it belongs to user ────
            var cartItem = await _context.CartItems
                .Include(ci => ci.Cart)
                .Include(ci => ci.Product)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ci => ci.Id == cartItemId
                                        && ci.Cart.UserId == userId);

            if (cartItem is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Cart item not found.");

            // ── Validate quantity ─────────────────────────────
            if (quantity <= 0)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Quantity must be at least 1. Use remove to delete item.");

            if (cartItem.Product.Stock < quantity)
                return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Fail(
                    $"Insufficient stock. Only {cartItem.Product.Stock} units available.");

            cartItem.Quantity = quantity;
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            var updatedCart = await GetCartWithItemsAsync(userId);
            return ApiResponseDto<CartResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapCart(updatedCart!),
                "Cart updated.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> RemoveItemAsync(
            int userId, int cartItemId)
        {
            var cartItem = await _context.CartItems
                .Include(ci => ci.Cart)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ci => ci.Id == cartItemId
                                        && ci.Cart.UserId == userId);

            if (cartItem is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Cart item not found.");

            _context.CartItems.Remove(cartItem);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true, "Item removed from cart.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> ClearCartAsync(int userId)
        {
            var cart = await _context.Carts
                .Include(c => c.CartItems)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.UserId == userId);

            if (cart is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Cart not found.");

            _context.CartItems.RemoveRange(cart.CartItems);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true, "Cart cleared.");
        }

        // ── Private helpers ───────────────────────────────────
        private async Task<Cart?> GetCartWithItemsAsync(int userId)
        {
            return await _context.Carts
                .Include(c => c.CartItems)
                    .ThenInclude(ci => ci.Product)
                        .ThenInclude(p => p.Images)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.UserId == userId);
        }

        private static CartResponseDto MapCart(Cart cart)
        {
            return new CartResponseDto
            {
                Id    = cart.Id,
                Items = cart.CartItems.Select(ci => new CartItemResponseDto
                {
                    Id                   = ci.Id,
                    ProductId            = ci.ProductId,
                    ProductName          = ci.Product.Name,
                    DosageForm           = ci.Product.DosageForm,
                    Strength             = ci.Product.Strength,
                    UnitPrice            = ci.Product.DiscountPrice
                                          ?? ci.Product.Price,
                    Quantity             = ci.Quantity,
                    RequiresPrescription = ci.Product.RequiresPrescription,
                    IsInStock            = ci.Product.Stock > 0,
                    ProductImageUrl      = ci.Product.Images
                        .FirstOrDefault(i => i.IsPrimary)?.ImageUrl
                        ?? ci.Product.Images.FirstOrDefault()?.ImageUrl
                }).ToList()
            };
        }
    }
}
5. Create Wishlist Service
Create file: Services/WishlistService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Wishlist;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles wishlist operations.
    /// Customers can save products to wishlist for later.
    /// </summary>
    public class WishlistService : IWishlistService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public WishlistService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<WishlistResponseDto>>> GetWishlistAsync(
            int userId)
        {
            var items = await _context.WishlistItems
                .Include(w => w.Product)
                    .ThenInclude(p => p.Images)
                .Where(w => w.UserId == userId)
                .OrderByDescending(w => w.CreatedAt)
                .ToListAsync();

            var result = items.Select(w => new WishlistResponseDto
            {
                Id                   = w.Id,
                ProductId            = w.ProductId,
                ProductName          = w.Product.Name,
                Price                = w.Product.Price,
                DiscountPrice        = w.Product.DiscountPrice,
                IsInStock            = w.Product.Stock > 0,
                RequiresPrescription = w.Product.RequiresPrescription,
                AddedAt              = w.CreatedAt,
                PrimaryImageUrl      = w.Product.Images
                    .FirstOrDefault(i => i.IsPrimary)?.ImageUrl
                    ?? w.Product.Images.FirstOrDefault()?.ImageUrl
            }).ToList();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<WishlistResponseDto>>.Ok(result);
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> AddToWishlistAsync(
            int userId, int productId)
        {
            // ── Check product exists ──────────────────────────
            var productExists = await _context.Products
                .AnyAsync(p => p.Id == productId && p.IsActive);

            if (!productExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Product not found.");

            // ── Check if already in wishlist ──────────────────
            var alreadyExists = await _context.WishlistItems
                .AnyAsync(w => w.UserId    == userId
                            && w.ProductId == productId);

            if (alreadyExists)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                    "Product is already in your wishlist.");

            _context.WishlistItems.Add(new WishlistItem
            {
                UserId    = userId,
                ProductId = productId
            });

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Product added to wishlist.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> RemoveFromWishlistAsync(
            int userId, int productId)
        {
            var item = await _context.WishlistItems
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(w => w.UserId    == userId
                                       && w.ProductId == productId);

            if (item is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Product not found in wishlist.");

            _context.WishlistItems.Remove(item);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Product removed from wishlist.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> IsInWishlistAsync(
            int userId, int productId)
        {
            var exists = await _context.WishlistItems
                .AnyAsync(w => w.UserId    == userId
                            && w.ProductId == productId);

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(exists);
        }
    }
}
6. Create Cart Controller
Create file: Controllers/CartController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Cart;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages the customer's shopping cart.
    /// All endpoints require authentication.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/cart")]
    [Authorize]
    public class CartController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ICartService _cartService;

        public CartController(ICartService cartService)
        {
            _cartService = cartService;
        }

        /// <summary>Get the current user's cart.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetCart()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _cartService.GetCartAsync(userId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Add a product to cart.</summary>
        [HttpPost("add")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> AddToCart(
            [FromBody] AddToCartRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _cartService.AddToCartAsync(userId, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Update quantity of a cart item.</summary>
        [HttpPut("items/{cartItemId:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateQuantity(
            int cartItemId, [FromBody] int quantity)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _cartService.UpdateQuantityAsync(
                userId, cartItemId, quantity);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Remove a specific item from cart.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("items/{cartItemId:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveItem(int cartItemId)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _cartService.RemoveItemAsync(userId, cartItemId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Clear all items from cart.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("clear")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ClearCart()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _cartService.ClearCartAsync(userId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }
    }
}

Step 8 — Orders & Checkout API
This is the most important business logic step. We handle the full checkout flow — coupon validation, prescription checking, stock deduction, order creation and order management.

1. Create Order Interface
Create file: Interfaces/IOrderService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Order;

namespace MediCare.API.Interfaces
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Contract for order operations.
    /// Customers place orders, Admin manages them.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IOrderService
    {
        // ── Customer ──────────────────────────────────────────
        Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> PlaceOrderAsync(
            int userId, PlaceOrderRequestDto request);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>> GetMyOrdersAsync(
            int userId, int pageNumber, int pageSize);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> GetMyOrderByIdAsync(
            int userId, int orderId);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> CancelOrderAsync(
            int userId, int orderId, string reason);

        // ── Admin ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>> GetAllOrdersAsync(
            int pageNumber, int pageSize, string? status);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> GetOrderByIdAsync(int orderId);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> UpdateOrderStatusAsync(
            int orderId, UpdateOrderStatusRequestDto request);
    }
}
2. Create Order Service
Create file: Services/OrderService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Order;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles the full order lifecycle:
    /// placement, status updates, cancellation.
    /// Validates stock, prescriptions and coupons at checkout.
    /// </summary>
    public class OrderService : IOrderService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public OrderService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // PLACE ORDER
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> PlaceOrderAsync(
            int userId, PlaceOrderRequestDto request)
        {
            // ── Get customer's cart ───────────────────────────
            var cart = await _context.Carts
                .Include(c => c.CartItems)
                    .ThenInclude(ci => ci.Product)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.UserId == userId);

            if (cart is null || !cart.CartItems.Any())
                return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Your cart is empty.");

            // ── Validate address belongs to user ──────────────
            var address = await _context.Addresses
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(a => a.Id     == request.AddressId
                                       && a.UserId == userId);

            if (address is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Delivery address not found.");

            // ── Validate each cart item ───────────────────────
            foreach (var item in cart.CartItems)
            {
                // Check stock
                if (item.Product.Stock < item.Quantity)
                    return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                        $"Insufficient stock for '{item.Product.Name}'. " +
                        $"Only {item.Product.Stock} units available.");

                // Check prescription requirement
                if (item.Product.RequiresPrescription)
                {
                    var hasApprovedPrescription = await _context.PrescriptionItems
                        .AnyAsync(pi =>
                            pi.ProductId  == item.ProductId &&
                            pi.IsApproved == true &&
                            pi.Prescription.UserId == userId &&
                            pi.Prescription.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Approved);

                    if (!hasApprovedPrescription)
                        return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                            $"'{item.Product.Name}' requires an approved " +
                            $"prescription. Please upload your prescription first.");
                }
            }

            // ── Calculate totals ──────────────────────────────
            var subtotal = cart.CartItems.Sum(ci =>
                (ci.Product.DiscountPrice ?? ci.Product.Price) * ci.Quantity);

            decimal discount       = 0;
            int?    couponId       = null;
            Coupon? coupon         = null;

            // ── Validate and apply coupon ─────────────────────
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.CouponCode))
            {
                coupon = await _context.Coupons
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c =>
                        c.Code     == request.CouponCode.ToUpper().Trim() &&
                        c.IsActive &&
                        c.ValidFrom <= DateTime.UtcNow &&
                        c.ValidTo   >= DateTime.UtcNow &&
                        c.UsedCount < c.UsageLimit);

                if (coupon is null)
                    return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                        "Invalid or expired coupon code.");

                if (subtotal < coupon.MinOrderAmount)
                    return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                        $"Minimum order amount for this coupon is " +
                        $"৳{coupon.MinOrderAmount}.");

                discount = coupon.DiscountType == DiscountTypes.Percentage
                    ? Math.Round(subtotal * coupon.Amount / 100, 2)
                    : coupon.Amount;

                couponId = coupon.Id;
            }

            // ── Calculate delivery charge ─────────────────────
            var deliveryCharge = subtotal - discount >= 500 ? 0 : 60;

            var total = subtotal - discount + deliveryCharge;

            // ── Create order ──────────────────────────────────
            var order = new Order
            {
                UserId         = userId,
                AddressId      = request.AddressId,
                CouponId       = couponId,
                Subtotal       = subtotal,
                Discount       = discount,
                DeliveryCharge = deliveryCharge,
                Total          = total,
                PaymentMethod  = request.PaymentMethod,
                PaymentStatus  = PaymentStatuses.Unpaid,
                OrderStatus    = OrderStatuses.Pending,
                Note           = request.Note
            };

            _context.Orders.Add(order);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Create order items + deduct stock ─────────────
            foreach (var item in cart.CartItems)
            {
                var orderItem = new OrderItem
                {
                    OrderId    = order.Id,
                    ProductId  = item.ProductId,
                    Quantity   = item.Quantity,
                    UnitPrice  = item.Product.DiscountPrice ?? item.Product.Price,
                    TotalPrice = (item.Product.DiscountPrice ?? item.Product.Price)
                                 * item.Quantity
                };

                _context.OrderItems.Add(orderItem);

                // Deduct stock
                item.Product.Stock -= item.Quantity;
            }

            // ── Create payment record ─────────────────────────
            var payment = new Payment
            {
                OrderId = order.Id,
                Method  = request.PaymentMethod,
                Amount  = total,
                Status  = request.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethods.COD
                    ? PaymentStatuses.Pending
                    : PaymentStatuses.Unpaid
            };

            _context.Payments.Add(payment);

            // ── Increment coupon usage ────────────────────────
            if (coupon is not null)
                coupon.UsedCount++;

            // ── Clear cart after order ────────────────────────
            _context.CartItems.RemoveRange(cart.CartItems);

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Send notification to customer ─────────────────
            var notification = new Notification
            {
                UserId  = userId,
                Title   = "Order Placed Successfully",
                Message = $"Your order #{order.Id} has been placed. " +
                          $"Total: ৳{total}.",
                Type    = NotificationTypes.Order
            };
            _context.Notifications.Add(notification);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Return full order ─────────────────────────────
            return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Ok(
                await MapOrderAsync(order.Id),
                "Order placed successfully!");
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — GET MY ORDERS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>>
            GetMyOrdersAsync(int userId, int pageNumber, int pageSize)
        {
            var query = _context.Orders
                .Where(o => o.UserId == userId)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAt);

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var orders = await query
                .Skip((pageNumber - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .Include(o => o.OrderItems)
                    .ThenInclude(oi => oi.Product)
                        .ThenInclude(p => p.Images)
                .Include(o => o.Address)
                .ToListAsync();

            var mapped = orders.Select(MapOrder).ToList();

            return ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>.Ok(
                new PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>
                {
                    Items      = mapped,
                    TotalCount = totalCount,
                    PageNumber = pageNumber,
                    PageSize   = pageSize
                });
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — GET SINGLE ORDER
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> GetMyOrderByIdAsync(
            int userId, int orderId)
        {
            var order = await GetOrderQueryable()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id     == orderId
                                       && o.UserId == userId);

            if (order is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Order not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Ok(MapOrder(order));
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — CANCEL ORDER
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> CancelOrderAsync(
            int userId, int orderId, string reason)
        {
            var order = await _context.Orders
                .Include(o => o.OrderItems)
                    .ThenInclude(oi => oi.Product)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id     == orderId
                                       && o.UserId == userId);

            if (order is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Order not found.");

            // ── Only pending/confirmed orders can be cancelled ─
            if (order.OrderStatus != OrderStatuses.Pending &&
                order.OrderStatus != OrderStatuses.Confirmed)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    $"Order cannot be cancelled — " +
                    $"current status is '{order.OrderStatus}'.");

            order.OrderStatus        = OrderStatuses.Cancelled;
            order.CancellationReason = reason;

            // ── Restore stock ─────────────────────────────────
            foreach (var item in order.OrderItems)
                item.Product.Stock += item.Quantity;

            // ── Notify customer ───────────────────────────────
            _context.Notifications.Add(new Notification
            {
                UserId  = userId,
                Title   = "Order Cancelled",
                Message = $"Your order #{order.Id} has been cancelled.",
                Type    = NotificationTypes.Order
            });

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Order cancelled successfully.");
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — GET ALL ORDERS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>>
            GetAllOrdersAsync(int pageNumber, int pageSize, string? status)
        {
            var query = GetOrderQueryable()
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAt);

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(status))
                query = (IOrderedQueryable<Order>)query
                    .Where(o => o.OrderStatus == status);

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var orders = await query
                .Skip((pageNumber - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>>.Ok(
                new PagedResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>
                {
                    Items      = orders.Select(MapOrder).ToList(),
                    TotalCount = totalCount,
                    PageNumber = pageNumber,
                    PageSize   = pageSize
                });
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — GET SINGLE ORDER
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> GetOrderByIdAsync(
            int orderId)
        {
            var order = await GetOrderQueryable()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == orderId);

            if (order is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Order not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Ok(MapOrder(order));
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — UPDATE ORDER STATUS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>> UpdateOrderStatusAsync(
            int orderId, UpdateOrderStatusRequestDto request)
        {
            var order = await _context.Orders
                .Include(o => o.OrderItems)
                    .ThenInclude(oi => oi.Product)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == orderId);

            if (order is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Order not found.");

            var previousStatus = order.OrderStatus;
            order.OrderStatus  = request.OrderStatus;

            if (request.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Cancelled)
            {
                order.CancellationReason = request.CancellationReason;

                // Restore stock on cancellation
                foreach (var item in order.OrderItems)
                    item.Product.Stock += item.Quantity;
            }

            // ── Mark payment as paid if delivered ─────────────
            if (request.OrderStatus  == OrderStatuses.Delivered &&
                order.PaymentMethod  == PaymentMethods.COD)
            {
                var payment = await _context.Payments
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.OrderId == orderId);

                if (payment is not null)
                {
                    payment.Status = PaymentStatuses.Paid;
                    payment.PaidAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
                    order.PaymentStatus = PaymentStatuses.Paid;
                }
            }

            // ── Notify customer of status change ──────────────
            _context.Notifications.Add(new Notification
            {
                UserId  = order.UserId,
                Title   = "Order Status Updated",
                Message = $"Your order #{order.Id} status has been " +
                          $"updated to '{request.OrderStatus}'.",
                Type    = NotificationTypes.Order
            });

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<OrderResponseDto>.Ok(
                await MapOrderAsync(orderId),
                "Order status updated successfully.");
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // PRIVATE HELPERS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        private IQueryable<Order> GetOrderQueryable() =>
            _context.Orders
                .Include(o => o.OrderItems)
                    .ThenInclude(oi => oi.Product)
                        .ThenInclude(p => p.Images)
                .Include(o => o.Address)
                .AsNoTracking();

        private static OrderResponseDto MapOrder(Order order) =>
            new()
            {
                Id                 = order.Id,
                OrderStatus        = order.OrderStatus,
                PaymentMethod      = order.PaymentMethod,
                PaymentStatus      = order.PaymentStatus,
                Subtotal           = order.Subtotal,
                Discount           = order.Discount,
                DeliveryCharge     = order.DeliveryCharge,
                Total              = order.Total,
                Note               = order.Note,
                CancellationReason = order.CancellationReason,
                CreatedAt          = order.CreatedAt,
                Address = new OrderAddressDto
                {
                    FullName    = order.Address.FullName,
                    Phone       = order.Address.Phone,
                    District    = order.Address.District,
                    Area        = order.Address.Area,
                    AddressLine = order.Address.AddressLine,
                    PostalCode  = order.Address.PostalCode
                },
                Items = order.OrderItems.Select(oi => new OrderItemResponseDto
                {
                    Id              = oi.Id,
                    ProductId       = oi.ProductId,
                    ProductName     = oi.Product.Name,
                    Quantity        = oi.Quantity,
                    UnitPrice       = oi.UnitPrice,
                    TotalPrice      = oi.TotalPrice,
                    ProductImageUrl = oi.Product.Images
                        .FirstOrDefault(i => i.IsPrimary)?.ImageUrl
                        ?? oi.Product.Images.FirstOrDefault()?.ImageUrl
                }).ToList()
            };

        private async Task<OrderResponseDto> MapOrderAsync(int orderId)
        {
            var order = await GetOrderQueryable()
                .FirstAsync(o => o.Id == orderId);
            return MapOrder(order);
        }
    }
}
3. Create Order Controller
Create file: Controllers/OrderController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Order;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages the full order lifecycle.
    /// Customers place and track orders.
    /// Admin manages all orders and updates status.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/orders")]
    [Authorize]
    public class OrderController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly IOrderService _orderService;

        public OrderController(IOrderService orderService)
        {
            _orderService = orderService;
        }

        // ── CUSTOMER ENDPOINTS ────────────────────────────────

        /// <summary>
        /// Place a new order from cart items.
        /// Validates stock and prescription requirements.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<IActionResult> PlaceOrder(
            [FromBody] PlaceOrderRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _orderService.PlaceOrderAsync(userId, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get all orders for the current customer.</summary>
        [HttpGet("my-orders")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetMyOrders(
            [FromQuery] int pageNumber = 1,
            [FromQuery] int pageSize   = 10)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _orderService
                .GetMyOrdersAsync(userId, pageNumber, pageSize);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get a specific order by ID for current customer.</summary>
        [HttpGet("my-orders/{orderId:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetMyOrderById(int orderId)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _orderService
                .GetMyOrderByIdAsync(userId, orderId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Cancel an order. Only Pending or Confirmed orders.</summary>
        [HttpPut("my-orders/{orderId:int}/cancel")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> CancelOrder(
            int orderId, [FromBody] string reason)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _orderService
                .CancelOrderAsync(userId, orderId, reason);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        // ── ADMIN ENDPOINTS ───────────────────────────────────

        /// <summary>
        /// Get all orders with optional status filter.
        /// Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpGet]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAllOrders(
            [FromQuery] int     pageNumber = 1,
            [FromQuery] int     pageSize   = 20,
            [FromQuery] string? status     = null)
        {
            var result = await _orderService
                .GetAllOrdersAsync(pageNumber, pageSize, status);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get a specific order by ID. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpGet("{orderId:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetOrderById(int orderId)
        {
            var result = await _orderService.GetOrderByIdAsync(orderId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Update order status.
        /// Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPut("{orderId:int}/status")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateOrderStatus(
            int orderId,
            [FromBody] UpdateOrderStatusRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var result = await _orderService
                .UpdateOrderStatusAsync(orderId, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }
    }
}
4. Register service in Program.cs
Add this line to the services section:

csharp
builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderService, OrderService>(); // ← add
