Or add address via API
A better way — add the address through the API directly. But we haven't built the Address API yet.

Let me give you a quick Address controller to unblock you right now.

Create file: Controllers/AddressController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Address;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages customer delivery addresses.
    /// All endpoints require authentication.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/addresses")]
    [Authorize]
    public class AddressController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public AddressController(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        /// <summary>Get all addresses for current user.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAll()
        {
            var userId    = User.GetUserId();
            var addresses = await _context.Addresses
                .Where(a => a.UserId == userId)
                .OrderByDescending(a => a.IsDefault)
                .ToListAsync();

            var result = addresses.Select(a => new AddressResponseDto
            {
                Id          = a.Id,
                FullName    = a.FullName,
                Phone       = a.Phone,
                District    = a.District,
                Area        = a.Area,
                AddressLine = a.AddressLine,
                PostalCode  = a.PostalCode,
                IsDefault   = a.IsDefault
            }).ToList();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<List<AddressResponseDto>>.Ok(result));
        }

        /// <summary>Add a new delivery address.</summary>
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
            [FromBody] AddressRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();

            // If this is set as default — unset others
            if (request.IsDefault)
            {
                var existing = await _context.Addresses
                    .Where(a => a.UserId == userId && a.IsDefault)
                    .ToListAsync();

                existing.ForEach(a => a.IsDefault = false);
            }

            var address = new Address
            {
                UserId      = userId,
                FullName    = request.FullName.Trim(),
                Phone       = request.Phone.Trim(),
                District    = request.District.Trim(),
                Area        = request.Area.Trim(),
                AddressLine = request.AddressLine.Trim(),
                PostalCode  = request.PostalCode,
                IsDefault   = request.IsDefault
            };

            _context.Addresses.Add(address);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<AddressResponseDto>.Ok(
                new AddressResponseDto
                {
                    Id          = address.Id,
                    FullName    = address.FullName,
                    Phone       = address.Phone,
                    District    = address.District,
                    Area        = address.Area,
                    AddressLine = address.AddressLine,
                    PostalCode  = address.PostalCode,
                    IsDefault   = address.IsDefault
                }, "Address added successfully."));
        }

        /// <summary>Update an existing address.</summary>
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Update(
            int id, [FromBody] AddressRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId  = User.GetUserId();
            var address = await _context.Addresses
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(a => a.Id     == id
                                       && a.UserId == userId);

            if (address is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Address not found."));

            if (request.IsDefault)
            {
                var existing = await _context.Addresses
                    .Where(a => a.UserId    == userId
                             && a.IsDefault == true
                             && a.Id        != id)
                    .ToListAsync();

                existing.ForEach(a => a.IsDefault = false);
            }

            address.FullName    = request.FullName.Trim();
            address.Phone       = request.Phone.Trim();
            address.District    = request.District.Trim();
            address.Area        = request.Area.Trim();
            address.AddressLine = request.AddressLine.Trim();
            address.PostalCode  = request.PostalCode;
            address.IsDefault   = request.IsDefault;

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Address updated successfully."));
        }

        /// <summary>Delete an address.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
        {
            var userId  = User.GetUserId();
            var address = await _context.Addresses
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(a => a.Id     == id
                                       && a.UserId == userId);

            if (address is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Address not found."));

            _context.Addresses.Remove(address);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Address deleted successfully."));
        }
    }
}

Step 9 — Prescription Upload & Admin Review API
This is the most unique and important feature of MediCare+. Customers upload prescriptions, admin reviews and approves them, then customers can order Rx medicines.

1. Create Prescription Interface
Create file: Interfaces/IPrescriptionService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Prescription;

namespace MediCare.API.Interfaces
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Contract for prescription operations.
    /// Customers upload — Admin reviews and responds.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IPrescriptionService
    {
        // ── Customer ──────────────────────────────────────────
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>> UploadAsync(
            int userId, UploadPrescriptionRequestDto request);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>>
            GetMyPrescriptionsAsync(int userId, int pageNumber, int pageSize);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>> GetMyPrescriptionByIdAsync(
            int userId, int prescriptionId);

        // ── Admin ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>>
            GetAllPrescriptionsAsync(
                int pageNumber, int pageSize, string? status);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>> GetPrescriptionByIdAsync(
            int prescriptionId);
        Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>> ReviewPrescriptionAsync(
            int prescriptionId, int adminId,
            ReviewPrescriptionRequestDto request);
    }
}
2. Create File Upload Helper
Create file: Helpers/FileUploadHelper.cs

csharp
namespace MediCare.API.Helpers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Validates uploaded prescription files.
    /// Checks file type, size and extension.
    /// </summary>
    public static class FileUploadHelper
    {
        private static readonly string[] AllowedExtensions =
            { ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".pdf" };

        private static readonly string[] AllowedMimeTypes =
        {
            "image/jpeg",
            "image/png",
            "application/pdf"
        };

        private const long MaxFileSizeBytes = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB

        public static (bool IsValid, string ErrorMessage) ValidateFile(
            IFormFile file)
        {
            if (file is null || file.Length == 0)
                return (false, "No file was uploaded.");

            if (file.Length > MaxFileSizeBytes)
                return (false, "File size must not exceed 5MB.");

            var extension = Path.GetExtension(file.FileName)
                               .ToLowerInvariant();

            if (!AllowedExtensions.Contains(extension))
                return (false,
                    "Only JPG, JPEG, PNG and PDF files are allowed.");

            if (!AllowedMimeTypes.Contains(file.ContentType.ToLower()))
                return (false, "Invalid file type.");

            return (true, string.Empty);
        }

        public static async Task<string> SaveFileLocallyAsync(
            IFormFile file, IWebHostEnvironment env)
        {
            var uploadsFolder = Path.Combine(
                env.WebRootPath ?? "wwwroot", "uploads", "prescriptions");

            Directory.CreateDirectory(uploadsFolder);

            var uniqueFileName = $"{Guid.NewGuid()}" +
                Path.GetExtension(file.FileName).ToLowerInvariant();

            var filePath = Path.Combine(uploadsFolder, uniqueFileName);

            using var stream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create);
            await file.CopyToAsync(stream);

            return $"/uploads/prescriptions/{uniqueFileName}";
        }
    }
}
3. Create Prescription Service
Create file: Services/PrescriptionService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Prescription;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles prescription upload and admin review workflow.
    /// Files are saved locally (Cloudinary integration in production).
    /// Admin approves/rejects and links medicines to prescription.
    /// </summary>
    public class PrescriptionService : IPrescriptionService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext      _context;
        private readonly IWebHostEnvironment _env;

        public PrescriptionService(
            AppDbContext context,
            IWebHostEnvironment env)
        {
            _context = context;
            _env     = env;
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — UPLOAD PRESCRIPTION
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>> UploadAsync(
            int userId, UploadPrescriptionRequestDto request)
        {
            // ── Validate file ─────────────────────────────────
            var (isValid, errorMessage) =
                FileUploadHelper.ValidateFile(request.File);

            if (!isValid)
                return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                    errorMessage);

            // ── Save file ─────────────────────────────────────
            var fileUrl = await FileUploadHelper
                .SaveFileLocallyAsync(request.File, _env);

            // ── Create prescription record ────────────────────
            var prescription = new Prescription
            {
                UserId      = userId,
                PatientName = request.PatientName.Trim(),
                FileUrl     = fileUrl,
                Message     = request.Message,
                Status      = PrescriptionStatuses.Pending
            };

            _context.Prescriptions.Add(prescription);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Notify admin — in production send email ───────
            // For now we create a notification for all admins
            var admins = await _context.Users
                .Where(u => u.Role == Roles.Admin)
                .ToListAsync();

            foreach (var admin in admins)
            {
                _context.Notifications.Add(new Notification
                {
                    UserId  = admin.Id,
                    Title   = "New Prescription Uploaded",
                    Message = $"Customer {prescription.PatientName} " +
                              $"has uploaded a new prescription " +
                              $"(#{prescription.Id}). Please review.",
                    Type    = NotificationTypes.Prescription
                });
            }

            // ── Notify customer ───────────────────────────────
            _context.Notifications.Add(new Notification
            {
                UserId  = userId,
                Title   = "Prescription Received",
                Message = $"Your prescription #{prescription.Id} has been " +
                          $"received and is pending review. We will notify " +
                          $"you once it has been reviewed.",
                Type    = NotificationTypes.Prescription
            });

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapPrescription(prescription),
                "Prescription uploaded successfully. " +
                "You will be notified once reviewed.");
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — GET MY PRESCRIPTIONS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>>
            GetMyPrescriptionsAsync(
                int userId, int pageNumber, int pageSize)
        {
            var query = _context.Prescriptions
                .Include(p => p.PrescriptionItems)
                    .ThenInclude(pi => pi.Product)
                .Where(p => p.UserId == userId)
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt);

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var prescriptions = await query
                .Skip((pageNumber - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>.Ok(
                new PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>
                {
                    Items      = prescriptions.Select(MapPrescription).ToList(),
                    TotalCount = totalCount,
                    PageNumber = pageNumber,
                    PageSize   = pageSize
                });
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CUSTOMER — GET SINGLE PRESCRIPTION
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>
            GetMyPrescriptionByIdAsync(int userId, int prescriptionId)
        {
            var prescription = await GetPrescriptionQueryable()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id     == prescriptionId
                                       && p.UserId == userId);

            if (prescription is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Prescription not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapPrescription(prescription));
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — GET ALL PRESCRIPTIONS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>>
            GetAllPrescriptionsAsync(
                int pageNumber, int pageSize, string? status)
        {
            var query = GetPrescriptionQueryable()
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt);

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(status))
                query = (IOrderedQueryable<Prescription>)query
                    .Where(p => p.Status == status);

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var prescriptions = await query
                .Skip((pageNumber - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>.Ok(
                new PagedResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>
                {
                    Items      = prescriptions.Select(MapPrescription).ToList(),
                    TotalCount = totalCount,
                    PageNumber = pageNumber,
                    PageSize   = pageSize
                });
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — GET SINGLE PRESCRIPTION
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>
            GetPrescriptionByIdAsync(int prescriptionId)
        {
            var prescription = await GetPrescriptionQueryable()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == prescriptionId);

            if (prescription is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Prescription not found.");

            return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Ok(
                MapPrescription(prescription));
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADMIN — REVIEW PRESCRIPTION
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>>
            ReviewPrescriptionAsync(
                int prescriptionId,
                int adminId,
                ReviewPrescriptionRequestDto request)
        {
            var prescription = await _context.Prescriptions
                .Include(p => p.PrescriptionItems)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == prescriptionId);

            if (prescription is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "Prescription not found.");

            // ── Cannot re-review completed prescriptions ──────
            if (prescription.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Ordered ||
                prescription.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Completed)
                return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                    $"Prescription is already '{prescription.Status}' " +
                    $"and cannot be reviewed again.");

            // ── Update prescription status ────────────────────
            prescription.Status     = request.Status;
            prescription.AdminNote  = request.AdminNote;
            prescription.ReviewedBy = adminId;
            prescription.ReviewedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            // ── Process approved items ────────────────────────
            if (request.ApprovedItems.Any())
            {
                // Remove old items if re-reviewing
                _context.PrescriptionItems
                    .RemoveRange(prescription.PrescriptionItems);

                foreach (var item in request.ApprovedItems)
                {
                    // Validate product exists
                    var productExists = await _context.Products
                        .AnyAsync(p => p.Id == item.ProductId
                                    && p.IsActive
                                    && p.RequiresPrescription);

                    if (!productExists)
                        return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
                            $"Product ID {item.ProductId} not found " +
                            $"or does not require a prescription.");

                    _context.PrescriptionItems.Add(new PrescriptionItem
                    {
                        PrescriptionId = prescriptionId,
                        ProductId      = item.ProductId,
                        Quantity       = item.Quantity,
                        Note           = item.Note,
                        IsApproved     = request.Status
                                         == PrescriptionStatuses.Approved
                    });
                }
            }

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            // ── Build notification message ────────────────────
            var notificationMessage = request.Status switch
            {
                PrescriptionStatuses.Approved =>
                    $"Your prescription #{prescriptionId} has been approved! " +
                    $"You can now order the approved medicines.",

                PrescriptionStatuses.Rejected =>
                    $"Your prescription #{prescriptionId} has been rejected. " +
                    $"Reason: {request.AdminNote ?? "See admin note."}",

                PrescriptionStatuses.NeedClarification =>
                    $"Your prescription #{prescriptionId} needs clarification. " +
                    $"Message: {request.AdminNote ?? "Please re-upload."}",

                _ => $"Your prescription #{prescriptionId} status has been " +
                     $"updated to '{request.Status}'."
            };

            // ── Notify customer ───────────────────────────────
            _context.Notifications.Add(new Notification
            {
                UserId  = prescription.UserId,
                Title   = "Prescription Update",
                Message = notificationMessage,
                Type    = NotificationTypes.Prescription
            });

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Ok(
                await GetMappedPrescriptionAsync(prescriptionId),
                "Prescription reviewed successfully.");
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // PRIVATE HELPERS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        private IQueryable<Prescription> GetPrescriptionQueryable() =>
            _context.Prescriptions
                .Include(p => p.PrescriptionItems)
                    .ThenInclude(pi => pi.Product)
                .AsNoTracking();

        private async Task<PrescriptionResponseDto> GetMappedPrescriptionAsync(
            int prescriptionId)
        {
            var prescription = await GetPrescriptionQueryable()
                .FirstAsync(p => p.Id == prescriptionId);
            return MapPrescription(prescription);
        }

        private static PrescriptionResponseDto MapPrescription(
            Prescription p) => new()
        {
            Id          = p.Id,
            PatientName = p.PatientName,
            FileUrl     = p.FileUrl,
            Message     = p.Message,
            Status      = p.Status,
            AdminNote   = p.AdminNote,
            CreatedAt   = p.CreatedAt,
            ReviewedAt  = p.ReviewedAt,
            ApprovedItems = p.PrescriptionItems?.Select(pi =>
                new PrescriptionItemResponseDto
                {
                    Id          = pi.Id,
                    ProductId   = pi.ProductId,
                    ProductName = pi.Product?.Name ?? string.Empty,
                    DosageForm  = pi.Product?.DosageForm,
                    Strength    = pi.Product?.Strength,
                    Quantity    = pi.Quantity,
                    Note        = pi.Note,
                    IsApproved  = pi.IsApproved
                }).ToList() ?? new()
        };
    }
}
4. Create Prescription Controller
Create file: Controllers/PrescriptionController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Prescription;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles prescription upload and admin review workflow.
    /// Upload uses multipart/form-data for file handling.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/prescriptions")]
    [Authorize]
    public class PrescriptionController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly IPrescriptionService _prescriptionService;

        public PrescriptionController(
            IPrescriptionService prescriptionService)
        {
            _prescriptionService = prescriptionService;
        }

        // ── CUSTOMER ENDPOINTS ────────────────────────────────

        /// <summary>
        /// Upload a prescription image or PDF.
        /// Uses multipart/form-data.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPost("upload")]
        [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Upload(
            [FromForm] UploadPrescriptionRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _prescriptionService
                .UploadAsync(userId, request);

            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Get all prescriptions for the current customer.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpGet("my-prescriptions")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetMyPrescriptions(
            [FromQuery] int pageNumber = 1,
            [FromQuery] int pageSize   = 10)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _prescriptionService
                .GetMyPrescriptionsAsync(userId, pageNumber, pageSize);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Get a specific prescription by ID for current customer.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpGet("my-prescriptions/{prescriptionId:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetMyPrescriptionById(
            int prescriptionId)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _prescriptionService
                .GetMyPrescriptionByIdAsync(userId, prescriptionId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        // ── ADMIN ENDPOINTS ───────────────────────────────────

        /// <summary>
        /// Get all prescriptions with optional status filter.
        /// Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpGet]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAll(
            [FromQuery] int     pageNumber = 1,
            [FromQuery] int     pageSize   = 20,
            [FromQuery] string? status     = null)
        {
            var result = await _prescriptionService
                .GetAllPrescriptionsAsync(pageNumber, pageSize, status);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Get a specific prescription. Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpGet("{prescriptionId:int}")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetById(int prescriptionId)
        {
            var result = await _prescriptionService
                .GetPrescriptionByIdAsync(prescriptionId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Review a prescription — approve, reject or
        /// request clarification. Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPut("{prescriptionId:int}/review")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Review(
            int prescriptionId,
            [FromBody] ReviewPrescriptionRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var adminId = User.GetUserId();
            var result  = await _prescriptionService
                .ReviewPrescriptionAsync(
                    prescriptionId, adminId, request);

            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }
    }
}

Apr 6
Claude responded: The error is clear:
The error is clear:

Product ID 1 not found or does not require a prescription.
The product exists but RequiresPrescription is set to false. Our review service only allows approving products that actually require a prescription.

Fix Option 1 — Update your product to require prescription
Run in pgAdmin:

sql
UPDATE "Products" 
SET "RequiresPrescription" = true 
WHERE "Id" = 1;
Then try the review again in Swagger.

Fix Option 2 — Create a new Rx product
Run in pgAdmin:

sql
INSERT INTO "Products" (
    "Name", "Slug", "SKU", "Description",
    "CategoryId", "BrandId",
    "Price", "Stock", "LowStockThreshold",
    "RequiresPrescription",
    "DosageForm", "Strength", "GenericName",
    "IsActive", "IsFeatured", "CreatedAt", "UpdatedAt"
) VALUES (
    'Azithromycin 500mg',
    'azithromycin-500mg-rx',
    'MED-RX-0001',
    'Antibiotic requiring prescription',
    1, 1,
    220.00, 50, 5,
    true,
    'Tablet', '500mg', 'Azithromycin',
    true, false, NOW(), NOW()
);
Then use that product's ID in the review request.

Fix Option 3 — Remove the Rx check in the review service
This is actually the most correct production fix — admin should be able to approve ANY product from a prescription, not just Rx-flagged ones. Sometimes admin adds OTC items that are part of the prescription.

Open Services/PrescriptionService.cs and find this block:

csharp
var productExists = await _context.Products
    .AnyAsync(p => p.Id == item.ProductId
                && p.IsActive
                && p.RequiresPrescription);

if (!productExists)
    return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
        $"Product ID {item.ProductId} not found " +
        $"or does not require a prescription.");
Replace with:

csharp
var productExists = await _context.Products
    .AnyAsync(p => p.Id == item.ProductId
                && p.IsActive);

if (!productExists)
    return ApiResponseDto<PrescriptionResponseDto>.Fail(
        $"Product ID {item.ProductId} not found or unavailable.");
Recommended — Do Fix 1 + Fix 3 together
Fix 1 — Update your test product:

sql
UPDATE "Products" 
SET "RequiresPrescription" = true,
    "UpdatedAt" = NOW()
WHERE "Id" = 1;
Fix 3 — Remove the strict Rx check in the service so admin has full flexibility.

Then run:

bash
dotnet run
And test the review again — it should approve successfully! 🚀

csharp
namespace MediCare.API.DTOs.Dashboard
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Admin dashboard stats shown on the main dashboard page.
    /// </summary>
    public class DashboardResponseDto
    {
        // ── Orders ────────────────────────────────────────────
        public int TotalOrders { get; set; }
        public int TodaysOrders { get; set; }
        public int PendingOrders { get; set; }
        public int ProcessingOrders { get; set; }
        public int DeliveredOrders { get; set; }
        public int CancelledOrders { get; set; }

        // ── Revenue ───────────────────────────────────────────
        public decimal TotalRevenue { get; set; }
        public decimal TodaysRevenue { get; set; }
        public decimal ThisMonthRevenue { get; set; }

        // ── Prescriptions ─────────────────────────────────────
        public int TotalPrescriptions { get; set; }
        public int PendingPrescriptions { get; set; }
        public int ApprovedPrescriptions { get; set; }
        public int RejectedPrescriptions { get; set; }

        // ── Products ──────────────────────────────────────────
        public int TotalProducts { get; set; }
        public int LowStockProducts { get; set; }
        public int OutOfStockProducts { get; set; }

        // ── Users ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        public int TotalCustomers { get; set; }
        public int NewCustomersToday { get; set; }

        // ── Top selling products ──────────────────────────────
        public List<TopProductDto> TopSellingProducts { get; set; } = new();

        // ── Recent orders ─────────────────────────────────────
        public List<RecentOrderDto> RecentOrders { get; set; } = new();
    }

    public class TopProductDto
    {
        public int    Id { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; }        = null!;
        public int    TotalSold { get; set; }
        public decimal Revenue { get; set; }
    }

    public class RecentOrderDto
    {
        public int     Id { get; set; }
        public string  CustomerName { get; set; } = null!;
        public decimal Total { get; set; }
        public string  Status { get; set; }       = null!;
        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }
    }
}

6. Create Dashboard Service
Create file: Services/DashboardService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Dashboard;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Computes real-time admin dashboard statistics.
    /// All queries run against the live database.
    /// </summary>
    public class DashboardService : IDashboardService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public DashboardService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<DashboardResponseDto>>
            GetDashboardStatsAsync()
        {
            var today     = DateTime.UtcNow.Date;
            var monthStart = new DateTime(
                DateTime.UtcNow.Year,
                DateTime.UtcNow.Month, 1);

            // ── Order stats ───────────────────────────────────
            var totalOrders = await _context.Orders.CountAsync();

            var todaysOrders = await _context.Orders
                .CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAt.Date == today);

            var pendingOrders = await _context.Orders
                .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Pending);

            var processingOrders = await _context.Orders
                .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Processing
                              || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Confirmed
                              || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Packed
                              || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Shipped);

            var deliveredOrders = await _context.Orders
                .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Delivered);

            var cancelledOrders = await _context.Orders
                .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Cancelled);

            // ── Revenue stats ─────────────────────────────────
            var totalRevenue = await _context.Orders
                .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid)
                .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

            var todaysRevenue = await _context.Orders
                .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
                         && o.CreatedAt.Date == today)
                .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

            var thisMonthRevenue = await _context.Orders
                .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
                         && o.CreatedAt     >= monthStart)
                .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

            // ── Prescription stats ────────────────────────────
            var totalPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions.CountAsync();

            var pendingPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
                .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Pending
                              || p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.UnderReview);

            var approvedPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
                .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Approved);

            var rejectedPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
                .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Rejected);

            // ── Product stats ─────────────────────────────────
            var totalProducts = await _context.Products
                .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive);

            var lowStockProducts = await _context.Products
                .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive
                              && p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold
                              && p.Stock > 0);

            var outOfStockProducts = await _context.Products
                .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive && p.Stock == 0);

            // ── Customer stats ────────────────────────────────
            var totalCustomers = await _context.Users
                .CountAsync(u => u.Role == Roles.Customer);

            var newCustomersToday = await _context.Users
                .CountAsync(u => u.Role         == Roles.Customer
                              && u.CreatedAt.Date == today);

            // ── Top selling products ──────────────────────────
            var topProducts = await _context.OrderItems
                .Include(oi => oi.Product)
                .GroupBy(oi => new
                {
                    oi.ProductId,
                    oi.Product.Name
                })
                .Select(g => new TopProductDto
                {
                    Id        = g.Key.ProductId,
                    Name      = g.Key.Name,
                    TotalSold = g.Sum(oi => oi.Quantity),
                    Revenue   = g.Sum(oi => oi.TotalPrice)
                })
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.TotalSold)
                .Take(5)
                .ToListAsync();

            // ── Recent orders ─────────────────────────────────
            var recentOrders = await _context.Orders
                .Include(o => o.User)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAt)
                .Take(10)
                .Select(o => new RecentOrderDto
                {
                    Id           = o.Id,
                    CustomerName = o.User.Name,
                    Total        = o.Total,
                    Status       = o.OrderStatus,
                    CreatedAt    = o.CreatedAt
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            var stats = new DashboardResponseDto
            {
                TotalOrders          = totalOrders,
                TodaysOrders         = todaysOrders,
                PendingOrders        = pendingOrders,
                ProcessingOrders     = processingOrders,
                DeliveredOrders      = deliveredOrders,
                CancelledOrders      = cancelledOrders,
                TotalRevenue         = totalRevenue,
                TodaysRevenue        = todaysRevenue,
                ThisMonthRevenue     = thisMonthRevenue,
                TotalPrescriptions   = totalPrescriptions,
                PendingPrescriptions = pendingPrescriptions,
                ApprovedPrescriptions = approvedPrescriptions,
                RejectedPrescriptions = rejectedPrescriptions,
                TotalProducts        = totalProducts,
                LowStockProducts     = lowStockProducts,
                OutOfStockProducts   = outOfStockProducts,
                TotalCustomers       = totalCustomers,
                NewCustomersToday    = newCustomersToday,
                TopSellingProducts   = topProducts,
                RecentOrders         = recentOrders
            };

            return ApiResponseDto<DashboardResponseDto>.Ok(stats);
        }
    }
}
7. Create Notification Service
Create file: Services/NotificationService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Notification;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles in-app notifications for customers and admins.
    /// </summary>
    public class NotificationService : INotificationService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public NotificationService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<NotificationResponseDto>>>
            GetMyNotificationsAsync(int userId)
        {
            var notifications = await _context.Notifications
                .Where(n => n.UserId == userId)
                .OrderByDescending(n => n.CreatedAt)
                .Take(50)
                .Select(n => new NotificationResponseDto
                {
                    Id          = n.Id,
                    Title       = n.Title,
                    Message     = n.Message,
                    Type        = n.Type,
                    IsRead      = n.IsRead,
                    RedirectUrl = n.RedirectUrl,
                    CreatedAt   = n.CreatedAt
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<NotificationResponseDto>>.Ok(
                notifications);
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> MarkAsReadAsync(
            int userId, int notificationId)
        {
            var notification = await _context.Notifications
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(n => n.Id     == notificationId
                                       && n.UserId == userId);

            if (notification is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Notification not found.");

            notification.IsRead = true;
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Notification marked as read.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> MarkAllAsReadAsync(int userId)
        {
            var notifications = await _context.Notifications
                .Where(n => n.UserId == userId && !n.IsRead)
                .ToListAsync();

            notifications.ForEach(n => n.IsRead = true);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "All notifications marked as read.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<int>> GetUnreadCountAsync(int userId)
        {
            var count = await _context.Notifications
                .CountAsync(n => n.UserId == userId && !n.IsRead);

            return ApiResponseDto<int>.Ok(count);
        }
    }
}
8. Create Review Service
Create file: Services/ReviewService.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Review;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using MediCare.API.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles product reviews and ratings.
    /// One review per customer per product.
    /// Customer can only review products they have ordered.
    /// </summary>
    public class ReviewService : IReviewService
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public ReviewService(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<List<ReviewResponseDto>>>
            GetProductReviewsAsync(int productId)
        {
            var reviews = await _context.Reviews
                .Include(r => r.User)
                .Where(r => r.ProductId  == productId
                         && r.IsApproved == true)
                .OrderByDescending(r => r.CreatedAt)
                .Select(r => new ReviewResponseDto
                {
                    Id        = r.Id,
                    Rating    = r.Rating,
                    Comment   = r.Comment,
                    UserName  = r.User.Name,
                    CreatedAt = r.CreatedAt
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<List<ReviewResponseDto>>.Ok(reviews);
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<ReviewResponseDto>> AddReviewAsync(
            int userId, ReviewRequestDto request)
        {
            // ── Check customer has ordered this product ────────
            var hasOrdered = await _context.OrderItems
                .AnyAsync(oi =>
                    oi.ProductId      == request.ProductId &&
                    oi.Order.UserId   == userId &&
                    oi.Order.OrderStatus == "Delivered");

            if (!hasOrdered)
                return ApiResponseDto<ReviewResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "You can only review products you have received.");

            // ── Check already reviewed ────────────────────────
            var alreadyReviewed = await _context.Reviews
                .AnyAsync(r => r.UserId    == userId
                            && r.ProductId == request.ProductId);

            if (alreadyReviewed)
                return ApiResponseDto<ReviewResponseDto>.Fail(
                    "You have already reviewed this product.");

            var review = new Review
            {
                UserId     = userId,
                ProductId  = request.ProductId,
                Rating     = request.Rating,
                Comment    = request.Comment,
                IsApproved = true
            };

            _context.Reviews.Add(review);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            var user = await _context.Users.FindAsync(userId);

            return ApiResponseDto<ReviewResponseDto>.Ok(
                new ReviewResponseDto
                {
                    Id        = review.Id,
                    Rating    = review.Rating,
                    Comment   = review.Comment,
                    UserName  = user?.Name ?? string.Empty,
                    CreatedAt = review.CreatedAt
                }, "Review submitted successfully.");
        }

        public async Task<ApiResponseDto<bool>> DeleteReviewAsync(
            int userId, int reviewId, bool isAdmin)
        {
            var review = isAdmin
                ? await _context.Reviews.FindAsync(reviewId)
                : await _context.Reviews
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(r => r.Id     == reviewId
                                           && r.UserId == userId);

            if (review is null)
                return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail("Review not found.");

            _context.Reviews.Remove(review);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Review deleted successfully.");
        }
    }
}

10. Create Notification Controller

csharp
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages in-app notifications for current user.
    /// All endpoints require authentication.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/notifications")]
    [Authorize]
    public class NotificationController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly INotificationService _notificationService;

        public NotificationController(
            INotificationService notificationService)
        {
            _notificationService = notificationService;
        }

        /// <summary>Get all notifications for current user.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetMyNotifications()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _notificationService
                .GetMyNotificationsAsync(userId);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Get unread notification count.</summary>
        [HttpGet("unread-count")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetUnreadCount()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _notificationService
                .GetUnreadCountAsync(userId);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Mark a specific notification as read.</summary>
        [HttpPut("{notificationId:int}/read")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> MarkAsRead(int notificationId)
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _notificationService
                .MarkAsReadAsync(userId, notificationId);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Mark all notifications as read.</summary>
        [HttpPut("read-all")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> MarkAllAsRead()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _notificationService
                .MarkAllAsReadAsync(userId);
            return Ok(result);
        }
    }
}
11. Create Review Controller
Create file: Controllers/ReviewController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Review;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using MediCare.API.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages product reviews and ratings.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/reviews")]
    public class ReviewController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly IReviewService _reviewService;

        public ReviewController(IReviewService reviewService)
        {
            _reviewService = reviewService;
        }

        /// <summary>Get all approved reviews for a product.</summary>
        [HttpGet("product/{productId:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetProductReviews(int productId)
        {
            var result = await _reviewService
                .GetProductReviewsAsync(productId);
            return Ok(result);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Add a review for a product.
        /// Customer must have received the product.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPost]
        [Authorize]
        public async Task<IActionResult> AddReview(
            [FromBody] ReviewRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var result = await _reviewService
                .AddReviewAsync(userId, request);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : BadRequest(result);
        }

        /// <summary>Delete a review. Customer or Admin.</summary>
        [HttpDelete("{reviewId:int}")]
        [Authorize]
        public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteReview(int reviewId)
        {
            var userId  = User.GetUserId();
            var isAdmin = User.GetUserRole() == Roles.Admin;
            var result  = await _reviewService
                .DeleteReviewAsync(userId, reviewId, isAdmin);
            return result.Success ? Ok(result) : NotFound(result);
        }
    }
}
12. Create User Management Controller
Create file: Controllers/UserController.cs

csharp
using MediCare.API.Data;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.Common;
using MediCare.API.DTOs.User;
using MediCare.API.Helpers;
using MediCare.API.Helpers.Constants;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MediCare.API.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages user profiles and admin user management.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/users")]
    [Authorize]
    public class UserController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public UserController(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        /// <summary>Get current user profile.</summary>
        [HttpGet("me")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetProfile()
        {
            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var user   = await _context.Users.FindAsync(userId);

            if (user is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "User not found."));

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<UserResponseDto>.Ok(
                new UserResponseDto
                {
                    Id              = user.Id,
                    Name            = user.Name,
                    Email           = user.Email,
                    Phone           = user.Phone,
                    Role            = user.Role,
                    ProfileImageUrl = user.ProfileImageUrl,
                    IsActive        = user.IsActive,
                    CreatedAt       = user.CreatedAt,
                    LastLoginAt     = user.LastLoginAt
                }));
        }

        /// <summary>Update current user profile.</summary>
        [HttpPut("me")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateProfile(
            [FromBody] UpdateProfileRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var user   = await _context.Users.FindAsync(userId);

            if (user is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "User not found."));

            // ── Check phone not taken by another user ──────────
            var phoneTaken = await _context.Users
                .AnyAsync(u => u.Phone == request.Phone.Trim()
                            && u.Id    != userId);

            if (phoneTaken)
                return BadRequest(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "This phone number is already used by another account."));

            user.Name            = request.Name.Trim();
            user.Phone           = request.Phone.Trim();
            user.ProfileImageUrl = request.ProfileImageUrl;

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Profile updated successfully."));
        }

        /// <summary>Change current user password.</summary>
        [HttpPut("me/change-password")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ChangePassword(
            [FromBody] ChangePasswordRequestDto request)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid)
                return BadRequest(ModelState);

            var userId = User.GetUserId();
            var user   = await _context.Users.FindAsync(userId);

            if (user is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "User not found."));

            // ── Verify current password ───────────────────────
            var isValid = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(
                request.CurrentPassword, user.PasswordHash);

            if (!isValid)
                return BadRequest(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Current password is incorrect."));

            user.PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt
                .HashPassword(request.NewPassword);

            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                "Password changed successfully."));
        }

        // ── ADMIN ENDPOINTS ───────────────────────────────────

        /// <summary>Get all customers. Admin only.</summary>
        [HttpGet]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> GetAllUsers(
            [FromQuery] int     pageNumber = 1,
            [FromQuery] int     pageSize   = 20,
            [FromQuery] string? search     = null)
        {
            var query = _context.Users
                .Where(u => u.Role == Roles.Customer)
                .AsQueryable();

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
            {
                var s = search.ToLower();
                query = query.Where(u =>
                    u.Name.ToLower().Contains(s) ||
                    u.Email.ToLower().Contains(s) ||
                    u.Phone.Contains(s));
            }

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var users = await query
                .OrderByDescending(u => u.CreatedAt)
                .Skip((pageNumber - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .ToListAsync();

            var result = new List<AdminUserResponseDto>();

            foreach (var user in users)
            {
                var totalOrders = await _context.Orders
                    .CountAsync(o => o.UserId == user.Id);

                var totalSpent = await _context.Orders
                    .Where(o => o.UserId       == user.Id
                             && o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid)
                    .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

                result.Add(new AdminUserResponseDto
                {
                    Id              = user.Id,
                    Name            = user.Name,
                    Email           = user.Email,
                    Phone           = user.Phone,
                    Role            = user.Role,
                    IsActive        = user.IsActive,
                    ProfileImageUrl = user.ProfileImageUrl,
                    CreatedAt       = user.CreatedAt,
                    LastLoginAt     = user.LastLoginAt,
                    TotalOrders     = totalOrders,
                    TotalSpent      = totalSpent
                });
            }

            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<PagedResponseDto<AdminUserResponseDto>>.Ok(
                new PagedResponseDto<AdminUserResponseDto>
                {
                    Items      = result,
                    TotalCount = totalCount,
                    PageNumber = pageNumber,
                    PageSize   = pageSize
                }));
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Toggle user active status. Admin only.
        /// </summary>
        [HttpPut("{userId:int}/toggle-status")]
        [Authorize(Roles = Roles.Admin)]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ToggleUserStatus(int userId)
        {
            var user = await _context.Users.FindAsync(userId);

            if (user is null)
                return NotFound(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "User not found."));

            if (user.Role == Roles.Admin)
                return BadRequest(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Fail(
                    "Cannot deactivate an admin account."));

            user.IsActive = !user.IsActive;
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

            var status = user.IsActive ? "activated" : "deactivated";
            return Ok(ApiResponseDto<bool>.Ok(true,
                $"User account {status} successfully."));
        }
    }
}

Claude responded: The error is clear:
The error is clear:

Cannot write DateTime with Kind=Unspecified to PostgreSQL type 'timestamp with time zone', only UTC is supported.
This is a common Npgsql issue. The monthStart variable is created as DateTime.Unspecified instead of DateTime.Utc.

Fix — Open Services/DashboardService.cs
Find these two lines at the top of GetDashboardStatsAsync():

csharp
var today      = DateTime.UtcNow.Date;
var monthStart = new DateTime(
    DateTime.UtcNow.Year,
    DateTime.UtcNow.Month, 1);
Replace with:

csharp
var now        = DateTime.UtcNow;
var today      = DateTime.SpecifyKind(now.Date, DateTimeKind.Utc);
var monthStart = DateTime.SpecifyKind(
    new DateTime(now.Year, now.Month, 1), DateTimeKind.Utc);
Also fix the newCustomersToday query — same issue:
Find:

csharp
var newCustomersToday = await _context.Users
    .CountAsync(u => u.Role         == Roles.Customer
                  && u.CreatedAt.Date == today);
Replace with:

csharp
var newCustomersToday = await _context.Users
    .CountAsync(u => u.Role == Roles.Customer
                  && u.CreatedAt >= today
                  && u.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1));
Also fix todaysOrders query:
Find:

csharp
var todaysOrders = await _context.Orders
    .CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAt.Date == today);
Replace with:

csharp
var todaysOrders = await _context.Orders
    .CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAt >= today
                  && o.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1));
Also fix todaysRevenue query:
Find:

csharp
var todaysRevenue = await _context.Orders
    .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
             && o.CreatedAt.Date == today)
Replace with:

csharp
var todaysRevenue = await _context.Orders
    .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
             && o.CreatedAt >= today
             && o.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1))
Your full fixed top section of GetDashboardStatsAsync():
csharp
public async Task<ApiResponseDto<DashboardResponseDto>>
    GetDashboardStatsAsync()
{
    var now        = DateTime.UtcNow;
    var today      = DateTime.SpecifyKind(now.Date, DateTimeKind.Utc);
    var monthStart = DateTime.SpecifyKind(
        new DateTime(now.Year, now.Month, 1), DateTimeKind.Utc);

    // ── Order stats ───────────────────────────────────
    var totalOrders = await _context.Orders.CountAsync();

    var todaysOrders = await _context.Orders
        .CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAt >= today
                      && o.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1));

    var pendingOrders = await _context.Orders
        .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Pending);

    var processingOrders = await _context.Orders
        .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Processing
                      || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Confirmed
                      || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Packed
                      || o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Shipped);

    var deliveredOrders = await _context.Orders
        .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Delivered);

    var cancelledOrders = await _context.Orders
        .CountAsync(o => o.OrderStatus == OrderStatuses.Cancelled);

    // ── Revenue stats ─────────────────────────────────
    var totalRevenue = await _context.Orders
        .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid)
        .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

    var todaysRevenue = await _context.Orders
        .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
                 && o.CreatedAt >= today
                 && o.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1))
        .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

    var thisMonthRevenue = await _context.Orders
        .Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatuses.Paid
                 && o.CreatedAt >= monthStart)
        .SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total) ?? 0;

    // ── Prescription stats ────────────────────────────
    var totalPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions.CountAsync();

    var pendingPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
        .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Pending
                      || p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.UnderReview);

    var approvedPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
        .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Approved);

    var rejectedPrescriptions = await _context.Prescriptions
        .CountAsync(p => p.Status == PrescriptionStatuses.Rejected);

    // ── Product stats ─────────────────────────────────
    var totalProducts = await _context.Products
        .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive);

    var lowStockProducts = await _context.Products
        .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive
                      && p.Stock <= p.LowStockThreshold
                      && p.Stock > 0);

    var outOfStockProducts = await _context.Products
        .CountAsync(p => p.IsActive && p.Stock == 0);

    // ── Customer stats ────────────────────────────────
    var totalCustomers = await _context.Users
        .CountAsync(u => u.Role == Roles.Customer);

    var newCustomersToday = await _context.Users
        .CountAsync(u => u.Role == Roles.Customer
                      && u.CreatedAt >= today
                      && u.CreatedAt < today.AddDays(1));

    // rest of the method stays the same...
Then run:

dotnet run

Should return all stats successfully! 🚀

Step 10 — Create the API client
Create file: lib/api.ts

typescript
import axios from 'axios';

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// ── Request interceptor — attach JWT token ─────────────────
api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('medicare_admin_token');
    if (token) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
  }
  return config;
});

// ── Response interceptor — handle 401 ─────────────────────
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.removeItem('medicare_admin_token');
        localStorage.removeItem('medicare_admin_user');
        window.location.href = '/login';
      }
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;
Step 11 — Create auth utility
Create file: lib/auth.ts

typescript
import { AuthResponse } from '@/types';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'medicare_admin_token';
const USER_KEY  = 'medicare_admin_user';

export const authUtils = {
  setAuth: (data: AuthResponse) => {
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, data.token);
    localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  },

  getToken: (): string | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  getUser: (): AuthResponse | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    const user = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
    return user ? JSON.parse(user) : null;
  },

  isAuthenticated: (): boolean => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
    const token = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    const user  = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
    if (!token || !user) return false;

    // Check token expiry
    const userData: AuthResponse = JSON.parse(user);
    const expiresAt = new Date(userData.expiresAt);
    return expiresAt > new Date();
  },

  isAdmin: (): boolean => {
    const user = authUtils.getUser();
    return user?.role === 'Admin';
  },

  logout: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    window.location.href = '/login';
  },
};
Step 12 — Create utility functions
Create file: lib/utils.ts

typescript
import { type ClassValue, clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

// ── Tailwind class merger ──────────────────────────────────
export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

// ── Currency formatter ────────────────────────────────────
export function formatCurrency(amount: number): string {
  return `৳${amount.toLocaleString('en-BD', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;
}

// ── Date formatter ────────────────────────────────────────
export function formatDate(date: string): string {
  return format(new Date(date), 'dd MMM yyyy');
}

export function formatDateTime(date: string): string {
  return format(new Date(date), 'dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm a');
}

// ── Time ago ──────────────────────────────────────────────
export function timeAgo(date: string): string {
  const now       = new Date();
  const past      = new Date(date);
  const diffMs    = now.getTime() - past.getTime();
  const diffMins  = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);
  const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMins / 60);
  const diffDays  = Math.floor(diffHours / 24);

  if (diffMins  < 1)  return 'just now';
  if (diffMins  < 60) return `${diffMins}m ago`;
  if (diffHours < 24) return `${diffHours}h ago`;
  if (diffDays  < 7)  return `${diffDays}d ago`;
  return formatDate(date);
}

// ── Truncate text ─────────────────────────────────────────
export function truncate(text: string, length: number): string {
  return text.length > length
    ? `${text.substring(0, length)}...`
    : text;
}

// ── Get initials from name ────────────────────────────────
export function getInitials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .split(' ')
    .map((n) => n[0])
    .join('')
    .toUpperCase()
    .substring(0, 2);
}

}

  outline-color: var(--ring);
}

  color: var(--foreground);

}

3. Create login page folder and file
Create file: app/(auth)/login/page.tsx

tsx
'use client';

import { useState }        from 'react';
import { useRouter }       from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm }         from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver }     from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { z }               from 'zod';
import { toast }           from 'sonner';
import { Eye, EyeOff, ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button }          from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input }           from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label }           from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription,
         CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import api                 from '@/lib/api';
import { authUtils }       from '@/lib/auth';
import { ApiResponse, AuthResponse } from '@/types';

// ── Validation schema ──────────────────────────────────────
const loginSchema = z.object({
  email:    z.string().email('Please enter a valid email address.'),
  password: z.string().min(6, 'Password must be at least 6 characters.'),
});

type LoginForm = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router                      = useRouter();
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading]   = useState(false);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<LoginForm>({
    resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema),
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: LoginForm) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.post<ApiResponse<AuthResponse>>(
        '/auth/login', data
      );

      if (!response.data.success) {
        toast.error(response.data.message);
        return;
      }

      const user = response.data.data!;

      // ── Only allow Admin ───────────────────────────────
      if (user.role !== 'Admin') {
        toast.error('Access denied. Admin accounts only.');
        return;
      }

      // ── Save auth data ─────────────────────────────────
      authUtils.setAuth(user);

      toast.success(`Welcome back, ${user.name}!`);
      router.push('/dashboard');

    } catch (error: any) {
      const message = error.response?.data?.message
        ?? 'Login failed. Please try again.';
      toast.error(message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 via-white to-blue-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">

      {/* Background pattern */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none">
        <div className="absolute -top-40 -right-40 w-80 h-80 bg-blue-100 rounded-full opacity-50 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-40 -left-40 w-80 h-80 bg-blue-100 rounded-full opacity-50 blur-3xl" />
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md relative z-10">

        {/* Logo / Brand */}
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 bg-blue-600 rounded-2xl mb-4 shadow-lg">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
            MediCare<span className="text-blue-600">+</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Admin Panel</p>
        </div>

        {/* Login Card */}
        <Card className="shadow-xl border-0 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <CardHeader className="space-y-1 pb-4">
            <CardTitle className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900">
              Sign in to your account
            </CardTitle>
            <CardDescription className="text-gray-500">
              Enter your admin credentials to continue
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>

          <CardContent>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4">

              {/* Email */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="email" className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                  Email address
                </Label>
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="admin@medicare.com"
                  autoComplete="email"
                  disabled={isLoading}
                  className={`h-11 ${errors.email ? 'border-red-500 focus-visible:ring-red-500' : ''}`}
                  {...register('email')}
                />
                {errors.email && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">
                    {errors.email.message}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Password */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="password" className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                  Password
                </Label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Input
                    id="password"
                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    placeholder="Enter your password"
                    autoComplete="current-password"
                    disabled={isLoading}
                    className={`h-11 pr-10 ${errors.password ? 'border-red-500 focus-visible:ring-red-500' : ''}`}
                    {...register('password')}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors"
                  >
                    {showPassword
                      ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" />
                      : <Eye    className="w-4 h-4" />
                    }
                  </button>
                </div>
                {errors.password && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">
                    {errors.password.message}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Submit */}
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="w-full h-11 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-semibold mt-2"
              >
                {isLoading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                    Signing in...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  'Sign In'
                )}
              </Button>

            </form>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Footer */}
        <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400 mt-6">
          MediCare+ Admin Panel · Secure Access Only
        </p>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

6. Create middleware for route protection
Create file: middleware.ts in the root of medicare-admin:

typescript
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server';

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const token = request.cookies.get('medicare_admin_token')?.value;
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  // ── Public routes ──────────────────────────────────────
  const publicRoutes = ['/login'];
  const isPublicRoute = publicRoutes.includes(pathname);

  // ── Redirect to login if no token ──────────────────────
  if (!isPublicRoute && !token) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/login', request.url));
  }

  // ── Redirect to dashboard if already logged in ─────────
  if (isPublicRoute && token) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url));
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    '/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)',
  ],
};
7. Update lib/auth.ts — save token to cookie too
Replace lib/auth.ts with:

typescript
import { AuthResponse } from '@/types';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'medicare_admin_token';
const USER_KEY  = 'medicare_admin_user';

export const authUtils = {
  setAuth: (data: AuthResponse) => {
    // Save to localStorage
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, data.token);
    localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));

    // Save token to cookie for middleware
    document.cookie = `${TOKEN_KEY}=${data.token}; path=/; max-age=${7 * 24 * 60 * 60}; SameSite=Lax`;
  },

  getToken: (): string | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  getUser: (): AuthResponse | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    const user = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
    return user ? JSON.parse(user) : null;
  },

  isAuthenticated: (): boolean => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
    const token = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    const user  = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
    if (!token || !user) return false;

    const userData: AuthResponse = JSON.parse(user);
    const expiresAt = new Date(userData.expiresAt);
    return expiresAt > new Date();
  },

  isAdmin: (): boolean => {
    const user = authUtils.getUser();
    return user?.role === 'Admin';
  },

  logout: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    // Clear cookie
    document.cookie = `${TOKEN_KEY}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
    window.location.href = '/login';
  },
};
